function zE(e, t) {
    const n = Math.abs(t.top - e.y)
      , r = Math.abs(t.bottom - e.y)
      , s = Math.abs(t.right - e.x)
      , i = Math.abs(t.left - e.x);

    case i:
        return "left";
    case s:
        return "right";
    case n:
        return "top";
    case r:
        return "bottom";

        throw new Error("unreachable")
    }
}
function VE(e, t, n=5) {

            x: e.x + n,
            y: e.y - n
        });

            x: e.x + n,

    }
    return r
}

const Ji = (e, t) => {

    case L.invalid_literal:

    case L.unrecognized_keys:

    case L.invalid_union_discriminator:

    case L.invalid_return_type:

        break;
    case L.too_small:

    case L.too_big:

      , a = parseInt(t.toFixed(s).replace(".", ""));

    }

    lt(t, n) {
        return this.setLimit("max", t, !1, te.toString(n))
    }
    setLimit(t, n, r, s) {
        return new ss({
            ...this._def,
            checks: [...this._def.checks, {
                kind: t,
                value: n,
                inclusive: r,
                message: te.toString(s)
            }]
        })
    }
    _addCheck(t) {
        return new ss({
            ...this._def,
            checks: [...this._def.checks, t]
        })
    }
    positive(t) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "min",
            value: BigInt(0),
            inclusive: !1,
            message: te.toString(t)
        })
    }
    negative(t) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "max",
            value: BigInt(0),
            inclusive: !1,
            message: te.toString(t)
        })
    }
    nonpositive(t) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "max",
            value: BigInt(0),
            inclusive: !0,
            message: te.toString(t)
        })
    }
    nonnegative(t) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "min",
            value: BigInt(0),
            inclusive: !0,
            message: te.toString(t)
        })
    }
    multipleOf(t, n) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "multipleOf",
            value: t,
            message: te.toString(n)
        })
    }
    get minValue() {
        let t = null;
        for (const n of this._def.checks)
            n.kind === "min" && (t === null || n.value > t) && (t = n.value);
        return t
    }
    get maxValue() {
        let t = null;
        for (const n of this._def.checks)
            n.kind === "max" && (t === null || n.value < t) && (t = n.value);
        return t
    }
}

class zs extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        if (this._def.coerce && (t.data = new Date(t.data)),
        this._getType(t) !== Z.date) {
            const i = this._getOrReturnCtx(t);
            return W(i, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.date,
                received: i.parsedType
            }),
            le
        }
        if (isNaN(t.data.getTime())) {
            const i = this._getOrReturnCtx(t);
            return W(i, {
                code: L.invalid_date
            }),
            le
        }
        const r = new jt;
        let s;
        for (const i of this._def.checks)
            i.kind === "min" ? t.data.getTime() < i.value && (s = this._getOrReturnCtx(t, s),
            W(s, {
                code: L.too_small,
                message: i.message,
                inclusive: !0,
                exact: !1,
                minimum: i.value,
                type: "date"
            }),
            r.dirty()) : i.kind === "max" ? t.data.getTime() > i.value && (s = this._getOrReturnCtx(t, s),
            W(s, {
                code: L.too_big,
                message: i.message,
                inclusive: !0,
                exact: !1,
                maximum: i.value,
                type: "date"
            }),
            r.dirty()) : xe.assertNever(i);
        return {
            status: r.value,
            value: new Date(t.data.getTime())
        }
    }
    _addCheck(t) {
        return new zs({
            ...this._def,
            checks: [...this._def.checks, t]
        })
    }
    min(t, n) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "min",
            value: t.getTime(),
            message: te.toString(n)
        })
    }
    max(t, n) {
        return this._addCheck({
            kind: "max",
            value: t.getTime(),
            message: te.toString(n)
        })
    }
    get minDate() {
        let t = null;
        for (const n of this._def.checks)
            n.kind === "min" && (t === null || n.value > t) && (t = n.value);
        return t != null ? new Date(t) : null
    }
    get maxDate() {
        let t = null;
        for (const n of this._def.checks)
            n.kind === "max" && (t === null || n.value < t) && (t = n.value);
        return t != null ? new Date(t) : null
    }

class Tn extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        const {ctx: n, status: r} = this._processInputParams(t)
          , s = this._def;
        if (n.parsedType !== Z.array)
            return W(n, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.array,
                received: n.parsedType
            }),
            le;
        if (s.exactLength !== null) {
            const a = n.data.length > s.exactLength.value
              , l = n.data.length < s.exactLength.value;
            (a || l) && (W(n, {
                code: a ? L.too_big : L.too_small,
                minimum: l ? s.exactLength.value : void 0,
                maximum: a ? s.exactLength.value : void 0,
                type: "array",
                inclusive: !0,
                exact: !0,
                message: s.exactLength.message
            }),
            r.dirty())
        }
        if (s.minLength !== null && n.data.length < s.minLength.value && (W(n, {
            code: L.too_small,
            minimum: s.minLength.value,
            type: "array",
            inclusive: !0,
            exact: !1,
            message: s.minLength.message
        }),
        r.dirty()),
        s.maxLength !== null && n.data.length > s.maxLength.value && (W(n, {
            code: L.too_big,
            maximum: s.maxLength.value,
            type: "array",
            inclusive: !0,
            exact: !1,
            message: s.maxLength.message
        }),
        r.dirty()),
        n.common.async)
            return Promise.all([...n.data].map( (a, l) => s.type._parseAsync(new Gn(n,a,n.path,l)))).then(a => jt.mergeArray(r, a));
        const i = [...n.data].map( (a, l) => s.type._parseSync(new Gn(n,a,n.path,l)));
        return jt.mergeArray(r, i)
    }
    get element() {
        return this._def.type
    }
    min(t, n) {
        return new Tn({
            ...this._def,
            minLength: {
                value: t,
                message: te.toString(n)
            }
        })
    }
    max(t, n) {
        return new Tn({
            ...this._def,
            maxLength: {
                value: t,
                message: te.toString(n)
            }
        })
    }
    length(t, n) {
        return new Tn({
            ...this._def,
            exactLength: {
                value: t,
                message: te.toString(n)
            }
        })
    }
    nonempty(t) {
        return this.min(1, t)
    }
}

function Js(e) {
    if (e instanceof Be) {
        const t = {};
        for (const n in e.shape) {
            const r = e.shape[n];
            t[n] = Kn.create(Js(r))
        }
        return new Be({
            ...e._def,
            shape: () => t
        })
    } else
        return e instanceof Tn ? new Tn({
            ...e._def,
            type: Js(e.element)
        }) : e instanceof Kn ? Kn.create(Js(e.unwrap())) : e instanceof os ? os.create(Js(e.unwrap())) : e instanceof Yn ? Yn.create(e.items.map(t => Js(t))) : e
}
class Be extends he {
    constructor() {
        super(...arguments),
        this._cached = null,
        this.nonstrict = this.passthrough,
        this.augment = this.extend
    }
    _getCached() {
        if (this._cached !== null)
            return this._cached;
        const t = this._def.shape()
          , n = xe.objectKeys(t);
        return this._cached = {
            shape: t,
            keys: n
        }
    }
    _parse(t) {
        if (this._getType(t) !== Z.object) {
            const u = this._getOrReturnCtx(t);
            return W(u, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.object,
                received: u.parsedType
            }),
            le
        }
        const {status: r, ctx: s} = this._processInputParams(t)
          , {shape: i, keys: a} = this._getCached()
          , l = [];
        if (!(this._def.catchall instanceof yr && this._def.unknownKeys === "strip"))
            for (const u in s.data)
                a.includes(u) || l.push(u);
        const c = [];
        for (const u of a) {
            const f = i[u]
              , h = s.data[u];
            c.push({
                key: {
                    status: "valid",
                    value: u
                },
                value: f._parse(new Gn(s,h,s.path,u)),
                alwaysSet: u in s.data
            })
        }
        if (this._def.catchall instanceof yr) {
            const u = this._def.unknownKeys;
            if (u === "passthrough")
                for (const f of l)
                    c.push({
                        key: {
                            status: "valid",
                            value: f
                        },
                        value: {
                            status: "valid",
                            value: s.data[f]
                        }
                    });
            else if (u === "strict")
                l.length > 0 && (W(s, {
                    code: L.unrecognized_keys,
                    keys: l
                }),
                r.dirty());
            else if (u !== "strip")
                throw new Error("Internal ZodObject error: invalid unknownKeys value.")
        } else {
            const u = this._def.catchall;
            for (const f of l) {
                const h = s.data[f];
                c.push({
                    key: {
                        status: "valid",
                        value: f
                    },
                    value: u._parse(new Gn(s,h,s.path,f)),
                    alwaysSet: f in s.data
                })
            }
        }
        return s.common.async ? Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
            const u = [];
            for (const f of c) {
                const h = await f.key
                  , m = await f.value;
                u.push({
                    key: h,
                    value: m,
                    alwaysSet: f.alwaysSet
                })
            }
            return u
        }
        ).then(u => jt.mergeObjectSync(r, u)) : jt.mergeObjectSync(r, c)
    }
    get shape() {
        return this._def.shape()
    }
    strict(t) {
        return te.errToObj,
        new Be({
            ...this._def,
            unknownKeys: "strict",
            ...t !== void 0 ? {
                errorMap: (n, r) => {
                    var s, i, a, l;
                    const c = (a = (i = (s = this._def).errorMap) === null || i === void 0 ? void 0 : i.call(s, n, r).message) !== null && a !== void 0 ? a : r.defaultError;
                    return n.code === "unrecognized_keys" ? {
                        message: (l = te.errToObj(t).message) !== null && l !== void 0 ? l : c
                    } : {
                        message: c
                    }
                }
            } : {}
        })
    }
    strip() {
        return new Be({
            ...this._def,
            unknownKeys: "strip"
        })
    }
    passthrough() {
        return new Be({
            ...this._def,
            unknownKeys: "passthrough"
        })
    }
    extend(t) {
        return new Be({
            ...this._def,
            shape: () => ({
                ...this._def.shape(),
                ...t
            })
        })
    }
    merge(t) {
        return new Be({
            unknownKeys: t._def.unknownKeys,
            catchall: t._def.catchall,
            shape: () => ({
                ...this._def.shape(),
                ...t._def.shape()
            }),
            typeName: oe.ZodObject
        })
    }
    setKey(t, n) {
        return this.augment({
            [t]: n
        })
    }
    catchall(t) {
        return new Be({
            ...this._def,
            catchall: t
        })
    }
    pick(t) {
        const n = {};
        return xe.objectKeys(t).forEach(r => {
            t[r] && this.shape[r] && (n[r] = this.shape[r])
        }
        ),
        new Be({
            ...this._def,
            shape: () => n
        })
    }
    omit(t) {
        const n = {};
        return xe.objectKeys(this.shape).forEach(r => {
            t[r] || (n[r] = this.shape[r])
        }
        ),
        new Be({
            ...this._def,
            shape: () => n
        })
    }
    deepPartial() {
        return Js(this)
    }
    partial(t) {
        const n = {};
        return xe.objectKeys(this.shape).forEach(r => {
            const s = this.shape[r];
            t && !t[r] ? n[r] = s : n[r] = s.optional()
        }
        ),
        new Be({
            ...this._def,
            shape: () => n
        })
    }
    required(t) {
        const n = {};
        return xe.objectKeys(this.shape).forEach(r => {
            if (t && !t[r])
                n[r] = this.shape[r];
            else {
                let i = this.shape[r];
                for (; i instanceof Kn; )
                    i = i._def.innerType;
                n[r] = i
            }
        }
        ),
        new Be({
            ...this._def,
            shape: () => n
        })
    }
    keyof() {
        return xw(xe.objectKeys(this.shape))
    }
}

class ba extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        const {ctx: n} = this._processInputParams(t)
          , r = this._def.options;
        function s(i) {
            for (const l of i)
                if (l.result.status === "valid")
                    return l.result;
            for (const l of i)
                if (l.result.status === "dirty")
                    return n.common.issues.push(...l.ctx.common.issues),
                    l.result;
            const a = i.map(l => new Xt(l.ctx.common.issues));
            return W(n, {
                code: L.invalid_union,
                unionErrors: a
            }),
            le
        }
        if (n.common.async)
            return Promise.all(r.map(async i => {
                const a = {
                    ...n,
                    common: {
                        ...n.common,
                        issues: []
                    },
                    parent: null
                };
                return {
                    result: await i._parseAsync({
                        data: n.data,
                        path: n.path,
                        parent: a
                    }),
                    ctx: a
                }
            }
            )).then(s);
        {
            let i;
            const a = [];
            for (const c of r) {
                const u = {
                    ...n,
                    common: {
                        ...n.common,
                        issues: []
                    },
                    parent: null
                }
                  , f = c._parseSync({
                    data: n.data,
                    path: n.path,
                    parent: u
                });
                if (f.status === "valid")
                    return f;
                f.status === "dirty" && !i && (i = {
                    result: f,
                    ctx: u
                }),
                u.common.issues.length && a.push(u.common.issues)
            }
            if (i)
                return n.common.issues.push(...i.ctx.common.issues),
                i.result;
            const l = a.map(c => new Xt(c));
            return W(n, {
                code: L.invalid_union,
                unionErrors: l
            }),
            le
        }
    }
    get options() {
        return this._def.options
    }
}

    typeName: oe.ZodUnion,
    ...ce(t)

const rr = e => e instanceof Na ? rr(e.schema) : e instanceof On ? rr(e.innerType()) : e instanceof Ca ? [e.value] : e instanceof is ? e.options : e instanceof Ea ? xe.objectValues(e.enum) : e instanceof _a ? rr(e._def.innerType) : e instanceof wa ? [void 0] : e instanceof ja ? [null] : e instanceof Kn ? [void 0, ...rr(e.unwrap())] : e instanceof os ? [null, ...rr(e.unwrap())] : e instanceof sm || e instanceof Pa ? rr(e.unwrap()) : e instanceof Ta ? rr(e._def.innerType) : [];
class du extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        const {ctx: n} = this._processInputParams(t);
        if (n.parsedType !== Z.object)
            return W(n, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.object,
                received: n.parsedType
            }),
            le;
        const r = this.discriminator
          , s = n.data[r]
          , i = this.optionsMap.get(s);
        return i ? n.common.async ? i._parseAsync({
            data: n.data,
            path: n.path,
            parent: n
        }) : i._parseSync({
            data: n.data,
            path: n.path,
            parent: n
        }) : (W(n, {
            code: L.invalid_union_discriminator,
            options: Array.from(this.optionsMap.keys()),
            path: [r]
        }),
        le)
    }
    get discriminator() {
        return this._def.discriminator
    }
    get options() {
        return this._def.options
    }
    get optionsMap() {
        return this._def.optionsMap
    }
    static create(t, n, r) {
        const s = new Map;
        for (const i of n) {
            const a = rr(i.shape[t]);
            if (!a.length)
                throw new Error(`A discriminator value for key \`${t}\` could not be extracted from all schema options`);
            for (const l of a) {
                if (s.has(l))
                    throw new Error(`Discriminator property ${String(t)} has duplicate value ${String(l)}`);
                s.set(l, i)
            }
        }
        return new du({
            typeName: oe.ZodDiscriminatedUnion,
            discriminator: t,
            options: n,
            optionsMap: s,
            ...ce(r)
        })
    }
}
function Rf(e, t) {
    const n = or(e)
      , r = or(t);
    if (e === t)
        return {
            valid: !0,
            data: e
        };
    if (n === Z.object && r === Z.object) {
        const s = xe.objectKeys(t)
          , i = xe.objectKeys(e).filter(l => s.indexOf(l) !== -1)
          , a = {
            ...e,
            ...t
        };
        for (const l of i) {
            const c = Rf(e[l], t[l]);
            if (!c.valid)
                return {
                    valid: !1
                };
            a[l] = c.data
        }
        return {
            valid: !0,
            data: a
        }
    } else if (n === Z.array && r === Z.array) {
        if (e.length !== t.length)
            return {
                valid: !1
            };
        const s = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < e.length; i++) {
            const a = e[i]
              , l = t[i]
              , c = Rf(a, l);
            if (!c.valid)
                return {
                    valid: !1
                };
            s.push(c.data)
        }
        return {
            valid: !0,
            data: s
        }
    } else
        return n === Z.date && r === Z.date && +e == +t ? {
            valid: !0,
            data: e
        } : {
            valid: !1
        }
}
class Sa extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        const {status: n, ctx: r} = this._processInputParams(t)
          , s = (i, a) => {
            if (Tf(i) || Tf(a))
                return le;
            const l = Rf(i.value, a.value);
            return l.valid ? ((Pf(i) || Pf(a)) && n.dirty(),
            {
                status: n.value,
                value: l.data
            }) : (W(r, {
                code: L.invalid_intersection_types
            }),
            le)
        }
        ;
        return r.common.async ? Promise.all([this._def.left._parseAsync({
            data: r.data,
            path: r.path,
            parent: r
        }), this._def.right._parseAsync({
            data: r.data,
            path: r.path,
            parent: r
        })]).then( ([i,a]) => s(i, a)) : s(this._def.left._parseSync({
            data: r.data,
            path: r.path,
            parent: r
        }), this._def.right._parseSync({
            data: r.data,
            path: r.path,
            parent: r
        }))
    }
}

class Yn extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        const {status: n, ctx: r} = this._processInputParams(t);
        if (r.parsedType !== Z.array)
            return W(r, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.array,
                received: r.parsedType
            }),
            le;
        if (r.data.length < this._def.items.length)
            return W(r, {
                code: L.too_small,
                minimum: this._def.items.length,
                inclusive: !0,
                exact: !1,
                type: "array"
            }),
            le;
        !this._def.rest && r.data.length > this._def.items.length && (W(r, {
            code: L.too_big,
            maximum: this._def.items.length,
            inclusive: !0,
            exact: !1,
            type: "array"
        }),
        n.dirty());
        const i = [...r.data].map( (a, l) => {
            const c = this._def.items[l] || this._def.rest;
            return c ? c._parse(new Gn(r,a,r.path,l)) : null
        }
        ).filter(a => !!a);
        return r.common.async ? Promise.all(i).then(a => jt.mergeArray(n, a)) : jt.mergeArray(n, i)
    }
    get items() {
        return this._def.items
    }
    rest(t) {
        return new Yn({
            ...this._def,
            rest: t
        })
    }
}

            )

                    return le;
                (u.status === "dirty" || f.status === "dirty") && n.dirty(),
                l.set(u.value, f.value)

class Vs extends he {
    _parse(t) {
        const {status: n, ctx: r} = this._processInputParams(t);
        if (r.parsedType !== Z.set)
            return W(r, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.set,
                received: r.parsedType
            }),
            le;
        const s = this._def;
        s.minSize !== null && r.data.size < s.minSize.value && (W(r, {
            code: L.too_small,
            minimum: s.minSize.value,
            type: "set",
            inclusive: !0,
            exact: !1,
            message: s.minSize.message
        }),
        n.dirty()),
        s.maxSize !== null && r.data.size > s.maxSize.value && (W(r, {
            code: L.too_big,
            maximum: s.maxSize.value,
            type: "set",
            inclusive: !0,
            exact: !1,
            message: s.maxSize.message
        }),
        n.dirty());
        const i = this._def.valueType;
        function a(c) {
            const u = new Set;
            for (const f of c) {
                if (f.status === "aborted")
                    return le;
                f.status === "dirty" && n.dirty(),
                u.add(f.value)
            }
            return {
                status: n.value,
                value: u
            }
        }
        const l = [...r.data.values()].map( (c, u) => i._parse(new Gn(r,c,r.path,u)));
        return r.common.async ? Promise.all(l).then(c => a(c)) : a(l)
    }
    min(t, n) {
        return new Vs({
            ...this._def,
            minSize: {
                value: t,
                message: te.toString(n)
            }
        })
    }
    max(t, n) {
        return new Vs({
            ...this._def,
            maxSize: {
                value: t,
                message: te.toString(n)
            }
        })
    }
    size(t, n) {
        return this.min(t, n).max(t, n)
    }
    nonempty(t) {
        return this.min(1, t)
    }
}

class Ei extends he {
    constructor() {
        super(...arguments),
        this.validate = this.implement
    }
    _parse(t) {
        const {ctx: n} = this._processInputParams(t);
        if (n.parsedType !== Z.function)
            return W(n, {
                code: L.invalid_type,
                expected: Z.function,
                received: n.parsedType
            }),
            le;
        function r(l, c) {
            return Tc({
                data: l,
                path: n.path,
                errorMaps: [n.common.contextualErrorMap, n.schemaErrorMap, _c(), Ji].filter(u => !!u),
                issueData: {
                    code: L.invalid_arguments,
                    argumentsError: c
                }
            })
        }
        function s(l, c) {
            return Tc({
                data: l,
                path: n.path,
                errorMaps: [n.common.contextualErrorMap, n.schemaErrorMap, _c(), Ji].filter(u => !!u),
                issueData: {
                    code: L.invalid_return_type,
                    returnTypeError: c
                }
            })
        }
        const i = {
            errorMap: n.common.contextualErrorMap
        }
          , a = n.data;
        if (this._def.returns instanceof to) {
            const l = this;
            return Ot(async function(...c) {
                const u = new Xt([])
                  , f = await l._def.args.parseAsync(c, i).catch(x => {
                    throw u.addIssue(r(c, x)),
                    u
                }
                )
                  , h = await Reflect.apply(a, this, f);
                return await l._def.returns._def.type.parseAsync(h, i).catch(x => {
                    throw u.addIssue(s(h, x)),
                    u
                }
                )
            })
        } else {
            const l = this;
            return Ot(function(...c) {
                const u = l._def.args.safeParse(c, i);
                if (!u.success)
                    throw new Xt([r(c, u.error)]);
                const f = Reflect.apply(a, this, u.data)
                  , h = l._def.returns.safeParse(f, i);
                if (!h.success)
                    throw new Xt([s(f, h.error)]);
                return h.data
            })
        }
    }
    parameters() {
        return this._def.args
    }
    returnType() {
        return this._def.returns
    }
    args(...t) {
        return new Ei({
            ...this._def,
            args: Yn.create(t).rest(Ts.create())
        })
    }
    returns(t) {
        return new Ei({
            ...this._def,
            returns: t
        })
    }
    implement(t) {
        return this.parse(t)
    }
    strictImplement(t) {
        return this.parse(t)
    }
    static create(t, n, r) {
        return new Ei({
            args: t || Yn.create([]).rest(Ts.create()),
            returns: n || Ts.create(),
            typeName: oe.ZodFunction,
            ...ce(r)
        })
    }
}

class is extends he {
    constructor() {

        Mo.set(this, void 0)
    }
    _parse(t) {
        if (typeof t.data != "string") {
            const n = this._getOrReturnCtx(t)
              , r = this._def.values;
            return W(n, {
                expected: xe.joinValues(r),
                received: n.parsedType,
                code: L.invalid_type
            }),
            le
        }
        if (Pc(this, Mo) || pw(this, Mo, new Set(this._def.values)),
        !Pc(this, Mo).has(t.data)) {
            const n = this._getOrReturnCtx(t)
              , r = this._def.values;
            return W(n, {
                received: n.data,
                code: L.invalid_enum_value,
                options: r
            }),
            le
        }
        return Ot(t.data)
    }

        return this._def.values
    }

        const t = {};
        for (const n of this._def.values)
            t[n] = n;
        return t
    }

            t[n] = n;
        return t
    }
    get Enum() {
        const t = {};
        for (const n of this._def.values)
            t[n] = n;
        return t
    }
    extract(t, n=this._def) {
        return is.create(t, {
            ...this._def,
            ...n
        })
    }
    exclude(t, n=this._def) {
        return is.create(this.options.filter(r => !t.includes(r)), {
            ...this._def,
            ...n
        })
    }

class On extends he {
    innerType() {
        return this._def.schema
    }
    sourceType() {
        return this._def.schema._def.typeName === oe.ZodEffects ? this._def.schema.sourceType() : this._def.schema
    }
    _parse(t) {
        const {status: n, ctx: r} = this._processInputParams(t)
          , s = this._def.effect || null
          , i = {
            addIssue: a => {
                W(r, a),
                a.fatal ? n.abort() : n.dirty()
            }
            ,
            get path() {
                return r.path
            }
        };
        if (i.addIssue = i.addIssue.bind(i),
        s.type === "preprocess") {
            const a = s.transform(r.data, i);
            if (r.common.async)
                return Promise.resolve(a).then(async l => {
                    if (n.value === "aborted")
                        return le;
                    const c = await this._def.schema._parseAsync({
                        data: l,
                        path: r.path,
                        parent: r
                    });
                    return c.status === "aborted" ? le : c.status === "dirty" || n.value === "dirty" ? mi(c.value) : c
                }
                );
            {
                if (n.value === "aborted")
                    return le;
                const l = this._def.schema._parseSync({
                    data: a,
                    path: r.path,
                    parent: r
                });
                return l.status === "aborted" ? le : l.status === "dirty" || n.value === "dirty" ? mi(l.value) : l
            }
        }
        if (s.type === "refinement") {
            const a = l => {
                const c = s.refinement(l, i);
                if (r.common.async)
                    return Promise.resolve(c);
                if (c instanceof Promise)
                    throw new Error("Async refinement encountered during synchronous parse operation. Use .parseAsync instead.");
                return l
            }
            ;
            if (r.common.async === !1) {
                const l = this._def.schema._parseSync({
                    data: r.data,
                    path: r.path,
                    parent: r
                });
                return l.status === "aborted" ? le : (l.status === "dirty" && n.dirty(),
                a(l.value),
                {
                    status: n.value,
                    value: l.value
                })
            } else
                return this._def.schema._parseAsync({
                    data: r.data,
                    path: r.path,
                    parent: r
                }).then(l => l.status === "aborted" ? le : (l.status === "dirty" && n.dirty(),
                a(l.value).then( () => ({
                    status: n.value,
                    value: l.value
                }))))
        }
        if (s.type === "transform")
            if (r.common.async === !1) {
                const a = this._def.schema._parseSync({
                    data: r.data,
                    path: r.path,
                    parent: r
                });
                if (!$s(a))
                    return a;
                const l = s.transform(a.value, i);
                if (l instanceof Promise)
                    throw new Error("Asynchronous transform encountered during synchronous parse operation. Use .parseAsync instead.");
                return {
                    status: n.value,
                    value: l
                }
            } else
                return this._def.schema._parseAsync({
                    data: r.data,
                    path: r.path,
                    parent: r
                }).then(a => $s(a) ? Promise.resolve(s.transform(a.value, i)).then(l => ({
                    status: n.value,
                    value: l
                })) : a);
        xe.assertNever(s)
    }
}
On.create = (e, t, n) => new On({
    schema: e,
    typeName: oe.ZodEffects,
    effect: t,
    ...ce(n)
});

function ww(e, t={}, n) {
    return e ? eo.create().superRefine( (r, s) => {

                    })

            })
        })]
    })

function Go(e) {
    return /^401: .*Unauthorized/.test(e.message)
}

                credentials: "include"
            });
            if (!w.ok) {

            }
            const S = await w.json();

            console.log(`📊 Data is array: ${Array.isArray(S)}, length: ${S.length}`),
            S
        }
        ,

    const h = Ae({

        }

        onSuccess: () => {

                queryKey: ["/api/payroll", i, l]
            }),

function AP({onSuccess: e}) {
    const {toast: t} = Ht()
      , [n,r] = g.useState(0)
      , {data: s=[]} = Bt({
        queryKey: ["/api/user/tasks"]
    })
      , i = Cc({
        resolver: Ec(RP),
        defaultValues: {
            date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
            startTime: "",
            endTime: "",
            project: "",
            clientName: "",
            notes: ""
        }
    })
      , {watch: a, setValue: l} = i
      , c = a("startTime")
      , u = a("endTime")
      , f = a("project");
    et.useEffect( () => {
        if (c && u) {
            const x = new Date(`2024-01-01 ${c}`)
              , y = new Date(`2024-01-01 ${u}`).getTime() - x.getTime()
              , j = Math.max(0, y / (1e3 * 60 * 60));
            r(j)
        } else
            r(0)
    }
    , [c, u]);
    const h = Ae({
        mutationFn: async x => {
            await Ie("POST", "/api/time-entries", x)
        }
        ,
        onSuccess: () => {
            i.reset({
                date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
                startTime: "",
                endTime: "",
                project: "",
                clientName: "",
                notes: ""
            }),
            r(0),
            e(),
            t({
                title: "Success",
                description: "Time entry added successfully"
            })
        }
        ,
        onError: x => {
            t({
                title: "Error",
                description: x.message,
                variant: "destructive"
            })
        }
    })
      , m = x => {
        if (n <= 0) {
            t({
                title: "Invalid Time",
                description: "End time must be after start time",
                variant: "destructive"
            });
            return
        }
        h.mutate(x)
    }
    ;
    return o.jsxs("div", {
        children: [o.jsx("div", {

            children: o.jsx("h4", {
                className: "text-md font-medium text-foreground",

            })

                    children: "Date"
                }), o.jsx(ve, {
                    id: "date",
                    type: "date",
                    ...i.register("date"),
                    className: "mt-1"
                }), i.formState.errors.date && o.jsx("p", {
                    className: "text-sm text-red-600 mt-1",
                    children: i.formState.errors.date.message
                })]
            }), o.jsxs("div", {
                className: "grid grid-cols-2 gap-4",
                children: [o.jsxs("div", {
                    children: [o.jsx(ae, {
                        htmlFor: "startTime",
                        children: "Start Time"
                    }), o.jsx(ve, {
                        id: "startTime",
                        type: "time",
                        ...i.register("startTime"),
                        className: "mt-1"
                    }), i.formState.errors.startTime && o.jsx("p", {
                        className: "text-sm text-red-600 mt-1",
                        children: i.formState.errors.startTime.message
                    })]
                }), o.jsxs("div", {
                    children: [o.jsx(ae, {
                        htmlFor: "endTime",
                        children: "End Time"
                    }), o.jsx(ve, {
                        id: "endTime",
                        type: "time",
                        ...i.register("endTime"),
                        className: "mt-1"
                    }), i.formState.errors.endTime && o.jsx("p", {
                        className: "text-sm text-red-600 mt-1",
                        children: i.formState.errors.endTime.message
                    })]
                })]
            }), o.jsxs("div", {
                children: [o.jsx(ae, {
                    htmlFor: "project",
                    children: "Project/Task"
                }), o.jsxs(Ra, {
                    value: f,
                    onValueChange: x => l("project", x),
                    children: [o.jsx(no, {

                        })

                    })]

                })]
            }), (f === "design" || f === "proposals") && o.jsxs("div", {

                }), o.jsx(ve, {

                    placeholder: "Enter client name..."
                }), i.formState.errors.clientName && o.jsx("p", {

                })]
            }), o.jsxs("div", {

                }), o.jsx(fm, {

function OP({timeEntries: e, isLoading: t, onUpdate: n}) {
    const {toast: r} = Ht()
      , [s,i] = g.useState(null)
      , [a,l] = g.useState({
        date: "",
        startTime: "",
        endTime: "",
        project: "",
        clientName: "",
        notes: ""
    })
      , c = Ae({
        mutationFn: async p => {
            await Ie("DELETE", `/api/time-entries/${p}`)
        }
        ,
        onSuccess: () => {
            n(),
            r({
                title: "Success",
                description: "Time entry deleted successfully"
            })
        }
        ,
        onError: p => {
            r({
                title: "Error",
                description: p.message,
                variant: "destructive"
            })
        }
    })
      , u = Ae({
        mutationFn: async ({id: p, ...d}) => {
            await Ie("PATCH", `/api/time-entries/${p}`, d)
        }
        ,
        onSuccess: () => {
            i(null),
            n(),
            r({
                title: "Success",
                description: "Time entry updated successfully"
            })
        }
        ,
        onError: p => {
            r({
                title: "Error",
                description: p.message,
                variant: "destructive"
            })
        }
    })
      , f = p => {
        i(p),
        l({
            date: p.date,
            startTime: p.startTime,
            endTime: p.endTime,

            clientName: p.clientName || "",
            notes: p.notes || ""
        })
    }
      , h = () => {

            id: s.id,
            ...a
        })
    }
      , m = p => {
        confirm("Are you sure you want to delete this time entry?") && c.mutate(p)
    }
      , x = p => new Date(p + "T00:00:00").toLocaleDateString("en-US", {
        month: "short",
        day: "numeric",
        year: "numeric"
    })
      , b = (p, d) => {
        const v = new Date(`2024-01-01 ${p}`).toLocaleTimeString("en-US", {
            hour: "numeric",
            minute: "2-digit",
            hour12: !0
        })
          , w = new Date(`2024-01-01 ${d}`).toLocaleTimeString("en-US", {
            hour: "numeric",
            minute: "2-digit",
            hour12: !0
        });
        return `${v} - ${w}`
    }
      , y = p => {
        switch (p) {
        case "production":
            return "bg-primary text-primary-foreground";
        case "design":
            return "bg-muted text-foreground";
        default:
            return "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground"
        }
    }
      , j = p => {
        switch (p) {
        case "production":
            return "Production";
        case "design":
            return "Design";

        }
    }
    ;

function Sl() {
    const {toast: e} = Ht()
      , {user: t, isLoading: n} = mo()
      , r = new Date
      , [s,i] = g.useState(r.getMonth() + 1)
      , [a,l] = g.useState(r.getFullYear());
    g.useEffect( () => {
        if (!n && !t) {
            e({
                title: "Unauthorized",
                description: "You are logged out. Please log in again...",
                variant: "destructive"
            }),
            setTimeout( () => {
                window.location.href = "/auth"
            }
            , 500);
            return
        }
    }
    , [t, n, e]);
    const {data: c=[], isLoading: u, refetch: f} = Bt({
        queryKey: ["/api/time-entries", a, s],
        queryFn: async () => {
            const y = new Date(a,s - 1,1)
              , j = new Date(a,s,0)
              , p = new URLSearchParams({
                startDate: y.toISOString().split("T")[0],
                endDate: j.toISOString().split("T")[0]
            })
              , d = await fetch(`/api/time-entries?${p}`);
            if (!d.ok)
                throw new Error("Failed to fetch time entries");
            return d.json()
        }
        ,
        retry: !1
    })
      , h = Array.isArray(c) ? c : []
      , m = h.reduce( (y, j) => y + parseFloat(j.totalHours || "0"), 0)
      , x = h.reduce( (y, j) => {
        var w;
        const p = parseFloat(j.totalHours || "0")
          , d = t == null ? void 0 : t.hourlyRate;

        return ((w = j.project) == null ? void 0 : w.toLowerCase()) === "production" && (v = 15),
        y + p * v
    }

    currentPassword: ut.string().min(1, "Current password is required"),
    newPassword: ut.string().min(8, "New password must be at least 8 characters"),
    confirmPassword: ut.string().min(1, "Please confirm your password")
}).refine(e => e.newPassword === e.confirmPassword, {
    message: "Passwords don't match",
    path: ["confirmPassword"]
});

function IP({taskCategories: e, currentUser: t}) {
    const {toast: n} = Ht()
      , r = eu()
      , [s,i] = g.useState([{
        id: "1",
        date: "",
        startTime: "",
        endTime: "",
        description: "",
        taskCategoryId: 1
    }])
      , a = () => {
        const m = (Math.max(...s.map(x => parseInt(x.id))) + 1).toString();
        i([...s, {
            id: m,
            date: "",
            startTime: "",
            endTime: "",
            description: "",
            taskCategoryId: 1
        }])
    }
      , l = m => {
        s.length > 1 && i(s.filter(x => x.id !== m))
    }
      , c = (m, x, b) => {
        i(s.map(y => y.id === m ? {
            ...y,
            [x]: b
        } : y))
    }
      , u = Ae({
        mutationFn: async m => {
            const x = [];
            for (const b of m) {
                const y = await Ie("/api/time-entries", "POST", b);
                x.push(y)
            }
            return x
        }
        ,
        onSuccess: () => {
            n({
                title: "Success!",
                description: `Created ${s.length} time entries successfully.`
            }),
            r.invalidateQueries({
                queryKey: ["/api/time-entries"]
            }),
            i([{
                id: "1",
                date: "",
                startTime: "",
                endTime: "",
                description: "",
                taskCategoryId: 1
            }])
        }
        ,
        onError: m => {
            n({
                title: "Error",
                description: m.message || "Failed to create time entries",
                variant: "destructive"
            })
        }
    })
      , f = () => {
        const m = s.filter(b => b.date && b.startTime && b.endTime);
        if (m.length === 0) {
            n({
                title: "No Valid Entries",
                description: "Please fill in at least one complete time entry",
                variant: "destructive"
            });
            return
        }
        const x = m.map(b => ({
            date: b.date,
            start_time: `${b.date}T${b.startTime}:00`,
            end_time: `${b.date}T${b.endTime}:00`,
            description: b.description || "Recovery entry",
            task_category_id: b.taskCategoryId
        }));
        u.mutate(x)
    }
      , h = () => {
        const m = `Date,Start Time,End Time,Description,Task Category
2025-07-01,09:00,17:00,Daily work,General Design
2025-07-02,09:00,17:00,Daily work,General Design
2025-07-03,09:00,17:00,Daily work,General Design`
          , x = new Blob([m],{
            type: "text/csv"
        })
          , b = window.URL.createObjectURL(x)
          , y = document.createElement("a");
        y.href = b,
        y.download = `${t.first_name}_${t.last_name}_time_recovery_template.csv`,
        y.click(),
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(b)
    }
    ;
    return o.jsxs(Le, {
        className: "w-full max-w-6xl mx-auto",
        children: [o.jsxs(Rt, {
            className: "bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950",
            children: [o.jsxs(At, {
                className: "text-red-800 dark:text-red-200 flex items-center gap-2",
                children: [o.jsx(Gp, {
                    className: "h-5 w-5"
                }), "Emergency Time Entry Recovery - ", t.first_name, " ", t.last_name]
            }), o.jsx(ns, {
                className: "text-red-700 dark:text-red-300",
                children: "Quick bulk entry system to recover your lost time entries. Fill in your work hours from July 1-22, 2025."
            }), o.jsx(G, {
                onClick: h,
                variant: "outline",
                size: "sm",
                className: "w-fit",
                children: "Download CSV Template"
            })]
        }), o.jsxs($e, {
            className: "p-6",
            children: [o.jsxs("div", {
                className: "space-y-4",
                children: [o.jsxs("div", {
                    className: "grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 font-semibold text-sm",
                    children: [o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-2",
                        children: "Date"
                    }), o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-2",
                        children: "Start Time"
                    }), o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-2",
                        children: "End Time"
                    }), o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-3",
                        children: "Description"
                    }), o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-2",
                        children: "Task Category"
                    }), o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-1",
                        children: "Actions"
                    })]
                }), s.map(m => o.jsxs("div", {
                    className: "grid grid-cols-12 gap-2 items-center",
                    children: [o.jsx("div", {
                        className: "col-span-2",
                        children: o.jsx(ve, {
                            type: "date",
                            value: m.date,
                            onChange: x => c(m.id, "date", x.target.value),
                            min: "2025-07-01",
                            max: "2025-07-22"
                        })
                    }), o.jsx("div", {

        });
    if (!e)
        return o.jsxs(Rp, {
            children: [o.jsx(Jn, {
                path: "/auth",
                component: nd
            }), o.jsx(Jn, {
                path: "*",
                children: o.jsx(nd, {})
            })]
        });
    if (e.mustResetPassword)
        return o.jsx(nd, {});
    const r = e.role === "admin"
      , s = !r || r && n;
    return o.jsxs(Rp, {
        children: [o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "/auth",
            children: s ? o.jsx(Sl, {}) : o.jsx(ud, {})
        }), o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "/profile",
            component: DP
        }), o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "/emergency-recovery",
            component: FP
        }), o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "/time-tracking",
            component: Sl
        }), o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "/admin",
            children: r ? o.jsx(ud, {}) : o.jsx(Sl, {})
        }), o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "/",
            children: s ? o.jsx(Sl, {}) : o.jsx(ud, {})
        }), o.jsx(Jn, {
            path: "*",
            component: XE
        })]
    })
}
function $P() {
    return o.jsx(mk, {
        client: Ve,
        children: o.jsx(qE, {
            children: o.jsx(GE, {
                children: o.jsxs(ZE, {
                    children: [o.jsx(wC, {}), o.jsx(LP, {})]
                })
            })
        })
    })
}
ax(document.getElementById("root")).render(o.jsx($P, {}));
